\"请重试或移除失败的附件后再继续。\" : \"附件或技能正在加载，请稍候。\"));\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        const missingSkill = [...text.matchAll(/@\\[skill:([^\\]]+)\\]/g)].some(match => !mentionReferences.some(reference => reference.skill?.skillId === match[1] && reference.skill.instruction));\r\n        if (missingSkill) {\r\n            toast.warning(\"专业技能尚未加载完整，请稍后重试或重新选择技能。\");\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        if (!text || busy || preparingRef.current || agentLaunch.busy || retryPreparingRef.current.size > 0) return;\r\n        const skills = selectedCreationReferences(text, mentionReferences).flatMap(reference => reference.skill ? [reference.skill] : []);\r\n        try {\r\n            await agentLaunch.launch({\r\n                prompt: text,\r\n                attachments,\r\n                skills,\r\n                models: { text: config.textModel, image: config.imageModel, video: config.videoModel },\r\n                confirmTools,\r\n                entryMode: agentIntent ? \"agent\" : mode,\r\n                requireTextModel: false,\r\n            });\r\n        } catch (reason) {\r\n            toast.error(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : \"创作草稿未能交给映雪画布，输入内容已保留。\");\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const composerProps = {\r\n        agentIntent,\r\n        onAgentIntentChange: () => { if (!preparingRef.current && !agentLaunch.busy) { setAgentIntent(true); setMode(\"text\"); } },\r\n        confirmTools,\r\n        onConfirmToolsChange: (confirmTools: boolean) => setAgentState({ confirmTools }),\r\n        onSelectSkill: selectSkill,\r\n        uploading,\r\n        uploadFailures,\r\n        inputIssues,\r\n        submissionBlocked,\r\n        onAddFiles: (files: File[]) => void addAttachments(files),\r\n        onRetryUploads: () => void addAttachments(uploadFailures.map(item => item.file)),\r\n        onDismissUploadFailure: (file: File) => setUploadFailures(current => current.filter(item => item.file !== file)),\r\n        mode,\r\n        prompt,\r\n        setPrompt: (value: string) => { if (!preparingRef.current && !agentLaunch.busy) setPrompt(value); },\r\n        busy: busy || agentLaunch.busy || preparing,\r\n        attachments,\r\n        referenceImageSize,\r\n        maxReferences,\r\n        references: mentionReferences,\r\n        onRemoveAttachment: removeAttachment,\r\n        onReorderAttachments: reorderAttachments,\r\n        onOpenLibrary: () => setLibraryOpen(true),\r\n        fileInputRef,\r\n        onFileChange: handleFileChange,\r\n        onModeChange: selectMode,\r\n        model: selectedModel,\r\n        modelRequirements,\r\n        imageProfile,\r\n        videoProfile,\r\n        config,\r\n        onModelChange: (value: string) => updateConfig(agentIntent || mode === \"text\" ? \"textModel\" : mode === \"image\" ? \"imageModel\" : \"videoModel\", value),\r\n        onAgentModelChange: (kind: \"text\" | \"image\" | \"video\", value: string) => updateConfig(kind === \"text\" ? \"textModel\" : kind === \"image\" ? \"imageModel\" : \"videoModel\", value),\r\n        ratio,\r\n        setRatio,\r\n        seconds,\r\n        setSeconds,\r\n        quality,\r\n        setQuality,\r\n        videoQuality,\r\n        setVideoQuality,\r\n        count,\r\n        setCount,\r\n        promptOptimizerProvider,\r\n        composerFocusRef,\r\n        placeholderOverride: viewMode === \"storyboard\" && composingNextShot ? `SC.${String(nextShotNumber).padStart(2, \"0\")} · 写下这一镜的镜头、画面或故事` : undefined,\r\n        onSubmit: () => void submit(),\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const visibleShot = shots[visibleShotIndex];\r\n    const visibleShotResultIndex = visibleShot?.result ? activeConversation.messages.indexOf(visibleShot.result) : -1;\r\n\r\n    const workspace = <div className=\"creation-home relative flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden\">\r\n            {isEmpty ? <>\r\n                <div className=\"creation-top-actions\">\r\n                    <Tooltip title=\"历史对话\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"查看历史对话\" aria-expanded={historyOpen} className=\"creation-top-action\" onClick={() => setHistoryOpen(true)}><History /></button></Tooltip>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <main ref={threadScrollRef} onScroll={handleThreadScroll} className=\"creation-empty-workspace creation-scrollbar\">\r\n                <CreationEmptyBanner />\r\n                <div className=\"creation-chat-intro\">\r\n                    <span className=\"creation-intro-signal\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\r\n                    <p>映雪 · AI 创作工作台</p>\r\n                    <h1>让你的下一个想法，<span className=\"creation-intro-emphasis\"><span className=\"is-pink\">在这里</span><span className=\"is-blue\">成为作品</span></span></h1>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <div className=\"creation-empty-composer\">\r\n                    <CreationComposer {...composerProps} variant=\"empty\" />\r\n                </div>\r\n                <CreationEmptySuggest\r\n                    onStartPrompt={(nextMode, prompt) => { selectMode(nextMode); setPrompt(prompt); window.requestAnimationFrame(() => composerFocusRef.current?.focus()); }}\r\n                    onOpenLibrary={() => { selectMode(\"image\"); setLibraryOpen(true); }}\r\n                />\r\n            </main>\r\n            </> : viewMode === \"chat\" ? modelDisplayName(config, item.model) : \"\"}\r\n                        onRetryFailure={() => retryFailedMessage(item, index)}\r\n                        onCreateVariant={() => createVariant(item, index)}\r\n                    />)}</div></section>\r\n                </main>\r\n                <section className=\"creation-thread-composer\"><CreationComposer {...composerProps} variant=\"thread\" /></section>\r\n            </div> : <div className=\"storyboard-workbench\">\r\n                <StoryboardToolbar\r\n                    shots={shots}\r\n                    activeIndex={visibleShotIndex}\r\n                    composing={composingNextShot}\r\n                    onSelect={(index) => { setSelectedShotIndex(index); setComposingNextShot(false); }}\r\n                    onBeginCompose={beginComposeNextShot}\r\n                    onCancelCompose={cancelComposeNextShot}\r\n                    onNewConversation={startNewConversation}\r\n                    onOpenHistory={() => setHistoryOpen(true)}\r\n                    viewMode={viewMode}\r\n                    onViewModeChange={setViewMode}\r\n                />\r\n                <main ref={threadScrollRef} onScroll={handleThreadScroll} className=\"storyboard-workbench-stage creation-scrollbar\">\r\n                    <div className=\"storyboard-workbench-stage-inner\">\r\n                        {composingNextShot ? <StoryboardNextShotCard shotNumber={nextShotNumber} onCancel={cancelComposeNextShot} /> : visibleShot ? <StoryboardShotCard\r\n                            shot={visibleShot}\r\n                            shotNumber={visibleShotIndex + 1}\r\n                            modelName={visibleShot.result?.model ? modelDisplayName(config, visibleShot.result.model) : \"\"}\r\n                            busy={busy || preparing || agentLaunch.busy || uploading || skillLoading}\r\n                            onRetryFailure={() => { if (visibleShotResultIndex >= 0 && visibleShot.result) retryFailedMessage(visibleShot.result, visibleShotResultIndex); }}\r\n                            onCreateVariant={() => { if (visibleShotResultIndex >= 0 && visibleShot.result) createVariant(visibleShot.result, visibleShotResultIndex); }}\r\n                        /> : null}\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </main>\r\n                <section className=\"storyboard-workbench-composer\">\r\n                    <CreationComposer {...composerProps} variant=\"thread\" />\r\n                </section>\r\n            </div>}\r\n        </div>;\r\n\r\n    return <>\r\n        {embedded ? <>\r\n            <div className=\"creation-home creation-lobby\">\r\n                <CreationComposer {...composerProps} onSubmit={() => void submitLobbyToCanvas()} variant=\"lobby\" />\r\n            </div>\r\n        </> : workspace}\r\n        <CreationHistoryDrawer open={historyOpen} conversations={historyConversations} activeId={activeConversation.id} onClose={() => setHistoryOpen(false)} onSelect={selectConversation} onDelete={confirmDeleteConversation} />\r\n        <AssetLibraryPickerModal\r\n            open={libraryOpen}\r\n            items={libraryItems}\r\n            categoryLabels={{ ...creationAssetCategoryLabels, ...externalAssetSources.categoryLabels }}\r\n            folders={externalAssetSources.folders}\r\n            initialSelectedIds={attachments.flatMap((item) => item.id.startsWith(\"asset:\") ? [item.id.slice(6)] : item.id.startsWith(\"external:\") ? [item.id] : [])}\r\n            upload={{ accept: creationUploadAccept(agentIntent ? \"text\" : mode), description: agentIntent || mode === \"text\" ? \"支持图片、视频、音频和常用文档；媒体会保存到素材库\" : `支持图片${mode === \"video\" ? \"、视频和音频\" : \"\"}，上传后保存到素材库`, onUpload: uploadLibraryAssets, external: { accept: \"image/*\", description: \"写入当前 Eagle 文件夹；Eagle 当前支持图片文件\", onUpload: (files, folderId) => externalAssetSources.uploadExternalFiles(files, folderId) } }}\r\n            onClose={() => setLibraryOpen(false)}\r\n            onConfirm={handleLibrarySelect}\r\n        />\r\n    </>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst creationAssetCategoryLabels: Record<string, string> = { all: \"全部素材\", character: \"角色\", environment: \"场景\", wardrobe: \"服饰\", prop: \"道具\", weapon: \"武器\", style: \"画风\", other: \"其他\" };\r\n\r\nfunction CreationHistoryDrawer({ open, conversations, activeId, onClose, onSelect, onDelete }: { open: boolean; conversations: CreationConversation[]; activeId: string; onClose: () => void; onSelect: (conversation: CreationConversation) => void; onDelete: (conversation: CreationConversation) => void }) {\r\n    const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState(\"\");\r\n\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (open) setKeyword(\"\");\r\n    }, [open]);\r\n\r\n    const visibleConversations = useMemo(() => {\r\n        const query = keyword.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n        if (!query) return conversations;\r\n        return conversations.filter((conversation) => {\r\n            const latest = conversationPreviewMessage(conversation);\r\n            const searchable = [\r\n                conversation.title,\r\n                ...conversation.messages.flatMap((message) => [message.content, displayCreationPrompt(message.content, message.references || [])]),\r\n                latest?.mode ? modeLabels[latest.mode] : \"创作\",\r\n                formatConversationTime(conversation.updatedAt),\r\n            ].filter(Boolean).join(\" \").toLowerCase();\r\n            return searchable.includes(query);\r\n        });\r\n    }, [conversations, keyword]);\r\n\r\n    return <Drawer open={open} onClose={onClose} placement=\"right\" size=\"min(440px, 100vw)\" closeIcon={<X className=\"size-4\" />} className=\"creation-history-drawer\" rootClassName=\"creation-history-drawer-root\" styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }} title={<div className=\"creation-history-title\"><span>历史对话</span><small>{conversations.length} 个对话</small></div>}>\r\n        <div className=\"creation-history-content\">\r\n            <label className=\"creation-history-search\">\r\n                <Search aria-hidden=\"true\" />\r\n                <input value={keyword} onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)} placeholder=\"搜索对话标题或内容\" aria-label=\"搜索历史对话\" />\r\n            </label>\r\n            {visibleConversations.length ? <ul className=\"creation-history-list\" aria-label=\"历史对话，按更新时间倒序排列\">\r\n                {visibleConversations.map((conversation) => {\r\n                    const latest = conversationPreviewMessage(conversation);\r\n                    const active = conversation.id === activeId;\r\n                    return <li key={conversation.id} className={active ? \"is-active\" : undefined}>\r\n                        <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-history-item-main\" aria-current={active ? \"page\" : undefined} onClick={() => onSelect(conversation)}>\r\n                            <span className=\"creation-history-time\"><time dateTime={conversation.updatedAt}>{formatConversationTime(conversation.updatedAt)}</time><em>{latest?.mode ? modeLabels[latest.mode] : \"创作\"}</em></span>\r\n                            <strong className=\"creation-history-item-heading\">{conversation.title.trim() || \"新创作\"}</strong>\r\n                            <span className=\"creation-history-snippet\">{latest ? displayCreationPrompt(latest.content, latest.references || []).trim() || \"还没有开始创作\" : \"还没有开始创作\"}</span>\r\n                        </button>\r\n                        <Tooltip title=\"删除对话\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-history-delete\" aria-label={`删除对话：${conversation.title.trim() || \"新创作\"}`} onClick={() => onDelete(conversation)}><Trash2 /></button></Tooltip>\r\n                    </li>;\r\n                })}\r\n            </ul> : <div className=\"creation-history-empty\">{keyword.trim() ? \"没有找到匹配的对话\" : \"暂无历史对话\"}</div>}\r\n        </div>\r\n    </Drawer>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationViewSwitch({ viewMode, onChange }: { viewMode: CreationViewMode; onChange: (mode: CreationViewMode) => void }) {\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-view-switch\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"创作视图\">\r\n        <button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={viewMode === \"chat\"} onClick={() => onChange(\"chat\")}><MessageSquareText />连续对话</button>\r\n        <button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={viewMode === \"storyboard\"} onClick={() => onChange(\"storyboard\")}><Clapperboard />镜头创作</button>\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationWorkspaceToolbar({ viewMode, onViewModeChange, onNewConversation, onOpenHistory }: { viewMode: CreationViewMode; onViewModeChange: (mode: CreationViewMode) => void; onNewConversation: () => void; onOpenHistory: () => void }) {\r\n    return <header className=\"creation-thread-toolbar\">\r\n        <CreationViewSwitch viewMode={viewMode} onChange={onViewModeChange} />\r\n        <div className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-actions\">\r\n            <Tooltip title=\"新建创作\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"新建创作\" className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-action\" onClick={onNewConversation}><Plus /></button></Tooltip>\r\n            <Tooltip title=\"历史对话\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"查看历史对话\" className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-action\" onClick={onOpenHistory}><History /></button></Tooltip>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </header>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMessageView({ item, modelName, onRetryFailure, onCreateVariant }: { item: CreationMessage; modelName: string; onRetryFailure: () => void; onCreateVariant: () => void }) {\r\n    if (item.role === \"user\") return <CreationUserMessage item={item} />;\r\n    const mode = item.mode || \"text\";\r\n    const stateLabel = item.status === \"pending\" ? \"生成中\" : item.status === \"cancelled\" ? \"已停止\" : item.status === \"error\" ? \"生成失败\" : \"\";\r\n    const heading = <><span className=\"creation-message-mark\"><Sparkles /></span><strong>{mode === \"image\" ? \"图像生成\" : mode === \"video\" ? \"视频生成\" : \"映雪 AI\"}</strong>{mode !== \"text\" ? <span className=\"creation-message-progress-copy\">{item.status === \"pending\" ? `映雪正在生成${mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}……` : item.status === \"done\" ? `你的${mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}已创建` : null}</span> : null}{modelName ? <span className=\"creation-message-model\">{modelName}</span> : null}{item.createdAt ? <time dateTime={item.createdAt}>{formatMessageTime(item.createdAt)}</time> : null}{stateLabel ? <span className={`creation-message-state is-${item.status}`}>{stateLabel}</span> : null}</>;\r\n    const toolStatus: GenerationToolStatus = item.status === \"pending\" ? \"running\" : item.status === \"error\" ? \"error\" : item.status === \"cancelled\" ? \"cancelled\" : \"completed\";\r\n    return <article className={`creation-assistant-message is-${mode}`}>\r\n        {mode === \"text\" ? <><div className=\"creation-message-heading\">{heading}</div>{item.reasoning ? <MessageReasoning reasoning={item.reasoning} isStreaming={item.status === \"streaming\"} /> : null}<div className=\"creation-message-content\">{item.content ? <AIMessageMarkdown isStreaming={item.status === \"streaming\"}>{item.content}</AIMessageMarkdown> : <span>正在生成…</span>}</div></> : <GenerationToolCard status={toolStatus} heading={heading}><MediaResult item={item} onRetryFailure={onRetryFailure} onCreateVariant={onCreateVariant} /></GenerationToolCard>}\r\n        {item.error && mode === \"text\" ? <div className=\"creation-message-error\"><span>{generationErrorMessage(item.error)}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}><RefreshCw />重新生成</button></div> : null}\r\n    </article>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationUserMessage({ item }: { item: CreationMessage }) {\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\">(\"image\");\r\n    const copyText = useCopyText();\r\n    const visiblePrompt = displayCreationPrompt(item.content, item.references || []);\r\n    return <article className=\"creation-user-message\">\r\n        <div className=\"creation-user-message-meta\"><span>你</span>{item.createdAt ? <time dateTime={item.createdAt}>{formatMessageTime(item.createdAt)}</time> : null}<Tooltip title=\"复制消息\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-user-message-copy\" aria-label=\"复制提示词\" onClick={() => copyText(visiblePrompt, \"提示词已复制\")}><Copy /></button></Tooltip></div>\r\n        <div className=\"creation-user-message-copy-wrap\"><p>{visiblePrompt}</p></div>\r\n        {item.references?.length ? <CreationMessageReferences references={item.references} /> : null}\r\n        {item.attachments?.length ? <div className=\"creation-user-message-attachments\">{item.attachments.map((attachment) => {\r\n            const kind = creationAttachmentKind(attachment);\r\n            const previewable = kind === \"image\" || kind === \"video\";\r\n            const url = attachment.previewUrl || (\"dataUrl\" in attachment ? attachment.dataUrl : attachment.url) || \"\";\r\n            return <button key={attachment.id} type=\"button\" className={!previewable ? \"is-file\" : undefined} onClick={() => { if (!previewable) return; setPreviewType(kind === \"video\" ? \"video\" : \"image\"); setPreviewUrl(kind === \"video\" ? attachment.url || \"\" : url); }} aria-label={previewable ? `预览 ${attachment.name || \"附件\"}` : attachment.name || \"附件\"} disabled={previewable && !url}>{kind === \"video\" ? <video src={attachment.url || \"\"} poster={url !== attachment.url ? url : undefined} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" /> : kind === \"image\" ? <img src={url} alt={attachment.name || \"附件\"} width={44} height={44} loading=\"lazy\" /> : kind === \"audio\" ? <Music2 /> : <FileText />}{previewable ? <span aria-hidden=\"true\"><Maximize2 /></span> : null}</button>;\r\n        })}</div> : null}\r\n        <CreationMediaPreviewModal url={previewUrl} type={previewType} onClose={() => setPreviewUrl(\"\")} />\r\n    </article>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction MediaResult({ item, onRetryFailure, onCreateVariant }: { item: CreationMessage; onRetryFailure: () => void; onCreateVariant: () => void }) {\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\">(\"image\");\r\n    const assets = useAssetStore((state) => state.assets);\r\n    const resultUrls = item.resultUrls || [];\r\n    const resultAssetIds = resultUrls.length ? creationResultAssetIds(assets, { messageId: item.id, taskIds: item.taskIds || [], resultUrls }) : [];\r\n    const canvasPath = creationCanvasHandoffPath(resultAssetIds) || \"/canvas\";\r\n    if (item.status === \"pending\") return <CreationMediaPending mode={item.mode || \"image\"} ratio={item.settings?.ratio} />;\r\n    if ((item.status === \"error\" || item.status === \"cancelled\") && !resultUrls.length) return <div className=\"creation-media-error\"><span>{item.status === \"cancelled\" ? item.content || \"已停止\" : generationErrorMessage(item.error || \"生成失败\")}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}><RefreshCw />重新生成</button></div>;\r\n    if (!resultUrls.length) return <div className=\"creation-media-empty\">没有返回可预览结果 <button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}>重试</button></div>;\r\n    const isVideo = item.mode === \"video\";\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-media-result\">\r\n        {isVideo ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-video-result\" onClick={() => { setPreviewType(\"video\"); setPreviewUrl(resultUrls[0]); }} aria-label=\"预览生成视频\"><video muted preload=\"metadata\" src={resultUrls[0]} /><span><Maximize2 />预览视频</span></button> : <div className=\"creation-image-result-grid\">{resultUrls.map((url) => <button key={url} type=\"button\" className=\"creation-image-result\" onClick={() => { setPreviewType(\"image\"); setPreviewUrl(url); }} aria-label=\"预览生成图片\"><img src={url} alt=\"生成结果\" /><span><Maximize2 /></span></button>)}</div>}\r\n        <div className=\"creation-media-actions\"><span>{isVideo ? \"视频结果\" : `${resultUrls.length} 张图片`}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onCreateVariant}><RefreshCw />生成同款</button><Link to={canvasPath}>{resultAssetIds.length ? \"添加到画布\" : \"打开画布\"}</Link>{resultUrls.map((url, index) => <a key={`${url}-download`} href={url} download>{resultUrls.length > 1 ? `下载 ${index + 1}` : <><Download />下载</>}</a>)}</div>\r\n        <CreationMediaPreviewModal url={previewUrl} type={previewType} onClose={() => setPreviewUrl(\"\")} />\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMediaPending({ mode, ratio }: { mode: CreationMode; ratio?: string }) {\r\n    return <div className={`creation-media-pending is-${mode}`} style={{ aspectRatio: creationMediaAspectRatio(ratio, mode) }} aria-live=\"polite\"><span className=\"creation-media-pending-icon\"><Sparkles /></span><span className=\"sr-only\">映雪正在生成{mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}</span></div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMessageReferences({ references }: { references: CreationReference[] }) {\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-user-message-references\" aria-label=\"本次引用\">{references.map((reference) => {\r\n        const Icon = reference.kind === \"skill\" ? Sparkles : reference.kind === \"image\" ? ImageIcon : reference.kind === \"video\" ? Film : reference.kind === \"audio\" ? Music2 : FileText;\r\n        return <span key={reference.id} className=\"creation-user-message-reference\">{reference.previewUrl && reference.kind === \"video\" ? <video src={reference.previewUrl} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" aria-label={reference.label} /> : reference.previewUrl && reference.kind === \"image\" ? <img src={reference.previewUrl} alt=\"\" /> : <Icon />}<span>{reference.label}</span></span>;\r\n    })}</div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMediaPreviewModal({ url, type, onClose }: { url: string; type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\"; onClose: () => void }) {\r\n    return <Modal open={Boolean(url)} title={null} footer={null} centered destroyOnHidden width={type === \"video\" ? \"min(1160px, calc(100vw - 32px))\" : type === \"audio\" ? \"min(560px, calc(100vw - 32px))\" : \"min(980px, calc(100vw - 32px))\"} onCancel={onClose} className=\"creation-media-preview-modal\" styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }}>{url ? type === \"video\" ? <video controls autoPlay className=\"creation-media-preview-video\" src={url} /> : type === \"audio\" ? <div className=\"creation-audio-preview\"><Music2 size={36} /><audio controls src={url} aria-label=\"参考音频试听\" /></div> : <img className=\"creation-media-preview-image\" src={url} alt=\"媒体预览\" /> : null}</Modal>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationAttachmentThumbnail({ item, onPreview, onRemove }: {\r\n    item: CreationAttachment;\r\n    onPreview: (type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\", url: string) => void;\r\n    onRemove: (id: string) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n    const kind = creationAttachmentKind(item);\r\n    const previewable = kind !== \"file\";\r\n    const url = (kind === \"video\" || kind === \"audio\" ? item.url : item.previewUrl) || \"\";\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-reference-card-content\">\r\n        {kind === \"file\" ? <a className=\"creation-reference-card-preview is-file\" href={item.url} download={item.name} aria-label={`下载原始附件 ${item.name}`}><span className=\"creation-chat-file-icon\"><FileText /><em>{item.name}</em></span></a> : <button type=\"button\" className={`creation-reference-card-preview${kind === \"audio\" ? \" is-file\" : \"\"}`} onClick={() => onPreview(kind, url)} aria-label={`${kind === \"audio\" ? \"试听\" : \"放大预览\"} ${item.name}`} disabled={!url}>\r\n            {kind === \"video\" ? <video src={item.url} poster={item.previewUrl !== item.url ? item.previewUrl : undefined} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" aria-label={item.name} /> : kind === \"image\" ? <img src={item.previewUrl} alt={item.name} /> : <span className=\"creation-chat-file-icon\">{kind === \"audio\" ? <Music2 /> : <FileText />}<em>{item.name}</em></span>}\r\n            {previewable ? <span aria-hidden=\"true\"><Maximize2 /></span> : null}\r\n        </button>}\r\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-card-remove\" onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); onRemove(item.id); }} aria-label={`移除 ${item.name}`}><X /></button>\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\ntype ComposerProps = {\r\n    variant: \"empty\" | \"thread\" | \"lobby\";\r\n    agentIntent: boolean;\r\n    onAgentIntentChange: () => void;\r\n    confirmTools: boolean;\r\n    onConfirmToolsChange: (value: boolean) => void;\r\n    onSelectSkill: (skill: Skill) => Promise<void>;\r\n    onAgentModelChange: (kind: \"text\" | \"image\" | \"video\", value: string) => void;\r\n    uploading: boolean;\r\n    uploadFailures: { file: File; error: string }[];\r\n    submissionBlocked: boolean;\r\n    inputIssues: string[];\r\n    onAddFiles: (files: File[]) => void;\r\n    onRetryUploads: () => void;\r\n    onDismissUploadFailure: (file: File) => void;\r\n    mode: CreationMode;\r\n    prompt: string;\r\n    setPrompt: (value: string) => void;\r\n    busy: boolean;\r\n    attachments: CreationAttachment[];\r\n    referenceImageSize?: { width: number; height: number };\r\n    maxReferences: number;\r\n    references: CreationReference[];\r\n    onRemoveAttachment: (id: string) => void;\r\n    onReorderAttachments: (attachments: CreationAttachment[]) => void;\r\n    onOpenLibrary: () => void;\r\n    fileInputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;\r\n    onFileChange: (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;\r\n    onModeChange: (mode: CreationMode) => void;\r\n    model: string;\r\n    modelRequirements: ModelRequirements;\r\n    videoProfile: VideoCapabilityConfig;\r\n    imageProfile: ImageCapabilityConfig;\r\n    config: ReturnType<typeof useEffectiveConfig>;\r\n    onModelChange: (value: string) => void;\r\n    ratio: string;\r\n    setRatio: (value: string) => void;\r\n    seconds: string;\r\n    setSeconds: (value: string) => void;\r\n    quality: string;\r\n    setQuality: (value: string) => void;\r\n    videoQuality: string;\r\n    setVideoQuality: (value: string) => void;\r\n    count: string;\r\n    setCount: (value: string) => void;\r\n    promptOptimizerProvider: PromptOptimizerProvider | null;\r\n    composerFocusRef: RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>;\r\n    placeholderOverride?: string;\r\n    onSubmit: () => void;\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction CreationComposer(props: ComposerProps) {\r\n    const [draggingFiles, setDraggingFiles] = useState(false);\r\n    const [lobbyExpanded, setLobbyExpanded] = useState(false);\r\n    const [modelsMenuOpen, setModelsMenuOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const [skillsMenuOpen, setSkillsMenuOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const [modesMenuOpen, setModesMenuOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const lobbyRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (props.variant !== \"lobby\") return;\r\n        const onPointerDown = (event: Event) => {\r\n            const target = event.target;\r\n            if (!(target instanceof Element) || lobbyRef.current?.contains(target)) return;\r\n            // Popovers and the native file chooser move focus outside the editor.\r\n            // Collapse only on a real outside click, never during a toolbar click.\r\n            if (target.closest(\".ant-popover, .ant-modal, .ant-select-dropdown\")) return;\r\n            setLobbyExpanded(false);\r\n        };\r\n        document.addEventListener(\"pointerdown\", onPointerDown, true);\r\n        return () => document.removeEventListener(\"pointerdown\", onPointerDown, true);\r\n    }, [props.variant]);\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\">(\"image\");\r\n    const [promptOptimizerOpen, setPromptOptimizerOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const attachmentTrackRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);\r\n    const cardDragRef = useRef<{ startX: number; startY: number; moved: boolean } | null>(null);\r\n    const suppressAttachmentClickRef = useRef(false);\r\n    const [trackState, setTrackState] = useState({ canScrollLeft: false, canScrollRight: false, isExpanded: false, isDragging: false });\r\n    const canSubmit = Boolean(props.prompt.trim()) && !props.busy && !props.submissionBlocked;\r\n    const creditsEnabled = useUserStore((state) => state.features.creditsEnabled);\r\n    const priceChannel = resolveModelChannel(props.config, props.model);\r\n    const canOptimizePrompt = !props.agentIntent && Boolean(props.promptOptimizerProvider) && (props.mode === \"image\" || props.mode === \"video\");\r\n    const optimizerReferences = props.references.filter((reference) => reference.active && reference.kind !== \"skill\");\r\n    const credits = requestCreditCost({\r\n        channelMode: priceChannel.scope === \"system\" ? \"remote\" : \"local\",\r\n        modelCosts: priceChannel.modelCosts,\r\n        model: modelOptionName(props.model),\r\n        count: props.mode === \"image\" ? props.count : 1,\r\n        seconds: props.mode === \"video\" ? props.seconds : 1,\r\n    });\r\n    const showCost = props.variant !== \"lobby\" && !props.agentIntent && creditsEnabled && credits !== null;\r\n    const formattedCredits = credits?.toLocaleString(\"zh-CN\", { maximumFractionDigits: 6 });\r\n    const actionLabel = props.busy ? \"正在处理\" : props.uploading ? \"附件处理中\" : props.variant === \"lobby\" ? \"进入映雪画布\" : props.agentIntent ? \"带入画布 Agent\" : showCost ? `预计消耗 ${formattedCredits} 积分，发送` : \"发送\";\r\n    const placeholder = props.mode === \"text\"\r\n        ? \"描述你的故事、角色或想继续讨论的创意\"\r\n        : props.mode === \"image\"\r\n            ? \"描述画面、人物、场景、构图与风格\"\r\n            : \"描述镜头内容、运动、光线与节奏\";\r\n    const emptyPlaceholder = \"输入你的镜头、画面或故事。也可以添加参考图开始创作\";\r\n    const imageReferencesSupported = props.imageProfile.references.maxImages > 0;\r\n    const referencesSupported = props.agentIntent || (props.mode === \"image\" ? imageReferencesSupported : props.mode !== \"video\" || props.videoProfile.references.maxImages + props.videoProfile.references.maxVideos + props.videoProfile.references.maxAudios > 0);\r\n    const canAddMoreReferences = referencesSupported && props.attachments.length < props.maxReferences;\r\n    const imageSettingsSupported = props.imageProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" || props.imageProfile.quality.supported || props.imageProfile.maxOutputs > 1;\r\n    const updateTrackScrollState = useCallback(() => {\r\n        const track = attachmentTrackRef.current;\r\n        if (!track) return;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({\r\n            ...current,\r\n            canScrollLeft: track.scrollLeft > 1,\r\n            canScrollRight: track.scrollLeft + track.clientWidth < track.scrollWidth - 1,\r\n        }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackMouseEnter = useCallback(() => {\r\n        if (window.matchMedia(\"(hover: hover)\").matches) setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {\r\n        if (!trackState.isDragging) setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: false }));\r\n    }, [trackState.isDragging]);\r\n    const handleTrackFocus = useCallback(() => {\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackBlur = useCallback((event: FocusEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        if (!trackState.isDragging && !event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) {\r\n            setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: false }));\r\n        }\r\n    }, [trackState.isDragging]);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        const touchOnly = window.matchMedia(\"(hover: none)\").matches;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: touchOnly || current.isDragging }));\r\n        updateTrackScrollState();\r\n    }, [props.attachments.length, updateTrackScrollState]);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        const frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(updateTrackScrollState);\r\n        return () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);\r\n    }, [trackState.isExpanded, updateTrackScrollState]);\r\n    const beginCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        if (event.button !== 0 || props.busy) return;\r\n        if ((event.target as HTMLElement).closest(\".creation-reference-card-remove\")) return;\r\n        cardDragRef.current = { startX: event.clientX, startY: event.clientY, moved: false };\r\n    };\r\n    const endCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        const drag = cardDragRef.current;\r\n        if (!drag) return;\r\n        cardDragRef.current = null;\r\n        if (drag.moved) {\r\n            suppressAttachmentClickRef.current = true;\r\n            window.setTimeout(() => { suppressAttachmentClickRef.current = false; }, 0);\r\n        }\r\n        if (event.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: false }));\r\n    };\r\n    const moveCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        const drag = cardDragRef.current;\r\n        if (!drag || drag.moved) return;\r\n        if (Math.hypot(event.clientX - drag.startX, event.clientY - drag.startY) <= 4) return;\r\n        drag.moved = true;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: true, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    };\r\n    const previewAttachment = (type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\", url: string) => {\r\n        if (suppressAttachmentClickRef.current || cardDragRef.current?.moved) return;\r\n        setPreviewType(type);\r\n        setPreviewUrl(url);\r\n    };\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (!canOptimizePrompt) setPromptOptimizerOpen(false);\r\n    }, [canOptimizePrompt]);\r\n\r\n    const scrollAttachmentTrack = (direction: -1 | 1) => {\r\n        const track = attachmentTrackRef.current;\r\n        if (!track) return;\r\n        track.scrollBy({ left: direction * Math.max(track.clientWidth * 0.72, 120), behavior: \"smooth\" });\r\n        window.setTimeout(updateTrackScrollState, 180);\r\n    };\r\n    const lobbyIsExpanded = props.variant === \"lobby\" && (lobbyExpanded || modelsMenuOpen || modesMenuOpen || skillsMenuOpen || Boolean(props.prompt.trim()) || props.attachments.length > 0 || draggingFiles);\r\n    const composer = <section ref={lobbyRef} className={`creation-chat-composer is-${props.variant} has-structured-input${draggingFiles ? \" is-file-dragging\" : \"\"}${lobbyIsExpanded ? \" is-expanded\" : \"\"}${props.attachments.length ? \" has-attachments\" : \"\"}`}\r\n        onFocusCapture={() => { if (props.variant === \"lobby\") setLobbyExpanded(true); }}\r\n        onPointerDown={event => {\r\n            if (props.variant !== \"lobby\" || !(event.target instanceof Element) || event.target.closest(\"button, a, input, textarea, [contenteditable='true'], [role='button']\")) return;\r\n            props.composerFocusRef.current?.focus();\r\n        }}\r\n        onDragOver={event => { if (event.dataTransfer.types.includes(\"Files\")) { event.preventDefault(); if (!props.busy) setDraggingFiles(true); } }}\r\n        onDragLeave={event => { if (!event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) setDraggingFiles(false); }}\r\n        onDrop={event => { if (!event.dataTransfer.files.length) return; event.preventDefault(); setDraggingFiles(false); if (!props.busy) props.onAddFiles(Array.from(event.dataTransfer.files)); }}\r\n        onPaste={event => { if (event.clipboardData.files.length && !props.busy) { event.preventDefault(); props.onAddFiles(Array.from(event.clipboardData.files)); } }}>\r\n        {draggingFiles && <div className=\"creation-drop-hint\">松开以添加参考素材</div>}\r\n        <div className=\"creation-chat-writing-surface\">\r\n            <input ref={props.fileInputRef} type=\"file\" hidden accept={creationUploadAccept(props.agentIntent ? \"text\" : props.mode)} multiple onChange={props.onFileChange} />\r\n            <div className=\"creation-chat-editor\">\r\n                <CanvasResourceMentionTextarea ref={props.composerFocusRef} value={props.prompt} references={props.references} mentionMenuWidth={400} sendOnEnter={props.variant === \"lobby\"} onChange={props.setPrompt} onSubmit={props.onSubmit} containerClassName=\"creation-chat-mention-container\" className=\"creation-chat-mention-editor creation-scrollbar\" style={{ color: \"var(--creation-text)\" }} placeholder={props.agentIntent ? \"说出你的创意，或者从一个 Skill 开始创作…\" : props.placeholderOverride || (props.variant === \"empty\" ? emptyPlaceholder : placeholder)} aria-label=\"创作提示词，可使用 @ 引用当前参考内容或技能\" spellCheck disabled={props.busy} />\r\n                {props.attachments.length || (props.variant !== \"lobby\" && canAddMoreReferences) ? <div className=\"creation-reference-track-wrapper\" inert={props.busy}>\r\n                    <div className=\"creation-reference-stack-shell\" onMouseEnter={handleTrackMouseEnter} onMouseLeave={handleTrackMouseLeave} onFocus={handleTrackFocus} onBlur={handleTrackBlur}>\r\n                        {trackState.canScrollLeft ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-track-button is-left\" onClick={() => scrollAttachmentTrack(-1)} aria-label=\"向左浏览参考内容\" title=\"向左浏览参考内容\"><ChevronLeft aria-hidden=\"true\" /></button> : null}\r\n                        <Reorder.Group<CreationAttachment[]>\r\n                            as=\"ul\"\r\n                            ref={attachmentTrackRef}\r\n                            className={`creation-reference-track${trackState.isExpanded ? \" is-expanded\" : \"\"}${trackState.isDragging ? \" is-dragging\" : \"\"}${props.attachments.length ? \"\" : \" is-empty\"}`}\r\n                            axis=\"x\"\r\n                            values={props.attachments}\r\n                            onReorder={props.onReorderAttachments}\r\n                            layoutScroll\r\n                            role=\"list\"\r\n                            aria-label=\"参考内容轨道\"\r\n                            onScroll={updateTrackScrollState}\r\n                        >\r\n                            {props.attachments.map((item) => <Reorder.Item<CreationAttachment>\r\n                                key={item.id}\r\n                                value={item}\r\n                                layout=\"position\"\r\n                                drag={!props.busy}\r\n                                className=\"creation-reference-stack-card\"\r\n                                onPointerDown={beginCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerMove={moveCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerUp={endCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerCancel={endCardDrag}\r\n                                onDragStart={() => setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: true, isExpanded: true }))}\r\n                                onDragEnd={() => setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: false, isExpanded: true }))}\r\n                            >\r\n                                <CreationAttachmentThumbnail item={item} onPreview={previewAttachment} onRemove={props.onRemoveAttachment} />\r\n                            </Reorder.Item>)}\r\n                            {canAddMoreReferences ? <li className=\"creation-reference-add-slot\"><Tooltip title=\"添加更多参考内容\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-add-button\" onClick={props.onOpenLibrary} disabled={props.busy} aria-label=\"添加更多参考内容\"><Plus aria-hidden=\"true\" /></button></Tooltip></li> : null}\r\n                        </Reorder.Group>\r\n                        {trackState.canScrollRight ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-track-button is-right\" onClick={() => scrollAttachmentTrack(1)} aria-label=\"向右浏览参考内容\" title=\"向右浏览参考内容\"><ChevronRight aria-hidden=\"true\" /></button> : null}\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div> : null}\r\n            </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n        {(props.uploading || props.uploadFailures.length > 0 || props.inputIssues.length > 0) && <div className=\"creation-input-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\r\n            {props.uploading && <span><LoaderCircle size={14} className=\"animate-spin\" />正在处理附件，完成后即可提交</span>}\r\n            {props.inputIssues.map(issue => <p key={issue} role=\"alert\">{issue}</p>)}\r\n            {props.uploadFailures.map((item, index) => <p key={`${item.file.name}:${index}`} role=\"alert\"><span>{item.file.name}：{item.error}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={() => props.onDismissUploadFailure(item.file)} aria-label={`移除失败附件 ${item.file.name}`}><X size={13} /></button></p>)}\r\n            {props.uploadFailures.length > 0 && <button type=\"button\" disabled={props.uploading} onClick={props.onRetryUploads}>重试失败附件</button>}\r\n        </div>}\r\n        <footer className=\"creation-chat-dock\">\r\n            <div className=\"creation-chat-controls\" inert={props.busy}>\r\n                {props.variant !== \"lobby\" ? <VoiceRecordingButton\r\n                    disabled={props.busy}\r\n                    onTranscribed={(text) => props.setPrompt(props.prompt.trim() ? `${props.prompt} ${text}` : text)}\r\n                /> : null}\r\n                <ModePicker mode={props.mode} onModeChange={props.onModeChange} agentIntent={props.agentIntent} onAgentIntentChange={props.onAgentIntentChange} onOpenChange={setModesMenuOpen} execution={props.variant === \"lobby\" ? { confirmTools: props.confirmTools, onChange: props.onConfirmToolsChange } : undefined} />\r\n                <Tooltip title=\"添加附件\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-chat-control\" onClick={() => props.fileInputRef.current?.click()} disabled={props.busy || props.uploading || !referencesSupported} aria-label=\"从本机上传附件\"><Paperclip /><span>附件</span></button></Tooltip>\r\n                <Tooltip title=\"选择 Skill\"><span className=\"creation-control-tooltip-anchor\"><CreationSkillPicker onOpenChange={setSkillsMenuOpen} disabled={props.busy || props.uploading} onSelect={props.onSelectSkill} /></span></Tooltip>\r\n                {props.variant !== \"lobby\" && canOptimizePrompt ? <Tooltip title=\"用 AI 优化提示词\">\r\n                    <button\r\n                        type=\"button\"\r\n                        className=\"creation-chat-control\"\r\n                        onClick={() => setPromptOptimizerOpen(true)}\r\n                        aria-label=\"优化提示词\"\r\n                        aria-expanded={promptOptimizerOpen}\r\n                        aria-haspopup=\"dialog\"\r\n                    >\r\n                        <WandSparkles />\r\n                        <span>优化</span>\r\n                    </button>\r\n                </Tooltip> : null}\r\n                {props.variant !== \"lobby\" ? <Tooltip title={!referencesSupported ? \"当前模型不支持参考媒体\" : \"从素材库选择参考内容\"}><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-chat-control\" onClick={props.onOpenLibrary} disabled={props.busy || !referencesSupported} aria-label=\"打开素材库选择参考内容\"><FolderOpen /><span>素材库</span></button></Tooltip> : null}\r\n                {props.variant === \"lobby\" || props.agentIntent ? <CreationModelsMenu config={props.config} onChange={props.onAgentModelChange} open={modelsMenuOpen} onOpenChange={setModelsMenuOpen} /> : <ModelPicker config={props.config} value={props.model} onChange={props.onModelChange} capability={props.mode} requirements={props.modelRequirements} className=\"creation-model-picker\" placeholder={`选择${modeLabels[props.mode]}模型`} showSelectedPrice variant=\"creation\" />}\r\n                {props.agentIntent && props.variant !== \"lobby\" ? <>\r\n                    <Popover trigger=\"click\" placement=\"bottom\" content={<div className=\"creation-agent-mode-menu\"><button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={props.confirmTools} onClick={() => props.onConfirmToolsChange(true)}><strong>逐步确认</strong><small>Agent 执行制作工具前由你确认</small></button><button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={!props.confirmTools} onClick={() => props.onConfirmToolsChange(false)}><strong>自动执行</strong><small>发送后连续调用工具，生成按模型计费</small></button></div>}><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-chat-control\" aria-label={`Agent 执行方式：${props.confirmTools ? \"逐步确认\" : \"自动执行\"}`} title=\"选择 Agent 执行方式\"><ShieldCheck /><span>{props.confirmTools ? \"逐步确认\" : \"自动执行\"}</span><ChevronDown /></button></Popover>\r\n                </> : props.variant !== \"lobby\" ? <>\r\n                    {props.mode === \"video\" || (props.mode === \"image\" && imageSettingsSupported) ? <GenerationSettingsMenu {...props} /> : null}\r\n                    {props.mode === \"video\" ? <DurationMenu profile={props.videoProfile} seconds={props.seconds} onChange={props.setSeconds} /> : null}\r\n                </> : null}\r\n            </div>\r\n            <Button\r\n                type=\"text\"\r\n                className={`canvas-node-composer-submit ${showCost ? \"has-cost\" : \"\"}`}\r\n                disabled={props.busy || !canSubmit}\r\n                style={{\r\n                    color: !props.busy && !canSubmit ? \"var(--creation-faint)\" : \"var(--creation-text)\",\r\n                    \"--canvas-composer-submit-action\": !props.busy && !canSubmit ? \"var(--creation-surface-hover)\" : \"var(--creation-text)\",\r\n                    \"--canvas-composer-submit-action-fg\": !props.busy && !canSubmit ? \"var(--creation-faint)\" : \"var(--creation-bg)\",\r\n                } as CSSProperties}\r\n                onClick={props.busy ? undefined : props.onSubmit}\r\n                aria-label={actionLabel}\r\n                title={actionLabel}\r\n            >\r\n                {showCost ? <span className=\"canvas-node-composer-submit-cost\"><CreditSymbol /><span>{formattedCredits}</span></span> : null}\r\n                <span className=\"canvas-node-composer-submit-action\" aria-hidden>{props.busy ? <LoaderCircle className=\"size-3 animate-spin\" /> : <ArrowUp className=\"size-3\" />}</span>\r\n            </Button>\r\n        </footer>\r\n        <CreationMediaPreviewModal url={previewUrl} type={previewType} onClose={() => setPreviewUrl(\"\")} />\r\n    </section>;\r\n\r\n    return (\r\n        <CanvasPromptOptimizerDrawer\r\n            open={promptOptimizerOpen}\r\n            prompt={props.prompt}\r\n            generationMode={props.mode === \"video\" ? \"video\" : \"image\"}\r\n            targetModel={modelOptionName(props.model) || props.model}\r\n            targetProtocol={priceChannel.modelCosts?.find((item) => item.model === modelOptionName(props.model))?.protocol || priceChannel.interfaceType}\r\n            config={props.config}\r\n            optimizerModel={props.config.textModel}\r\n            references={optimizerReferences}\r\n            provider={props.promptOptimizerProvider}\r\n            onClose={() => setPromptOptimizerOpen(false)}\r\n            onApply={props.setPrompt}\r\n        >\r\n            {composer}\r\n        </CanvasPromptOptimizerDrawer>\r\n    );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction ModePicker({ mode, onModeChange, agentIntent, onAgentIntentChange, onOpenChange, execution }: { mode: CreationMode; onModeChange: (mode: GenerationSettingsMenu(props: ComposerProps) {\r\n    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const [customRatioOpen, setCustomRatioOpen] = useState(!ratioOptions.some((option) => option.value === props.ratio));\r\n    const activeQualityOptions = props.imageProfile.quality.values.map((value) => qualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === value) || { value, label: value.toUpperCase(), description: \"模型支持的质量/分辨率\" });\r\n    const qualityLabel = activeQualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === props.quality)?.label || qualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === props.quality)?.label || props.quality || \"自动\";\r\n    // 尺寸/比例/分辨率选项取同显示名分组内全部模型的并集，路由模型只决定发送参数。\r\n    const mergedProfile = mergedImageCapabilityConfig(props.config, props.model || props.config.imageModel);\r\n    const usesImageResolutionPicker = props.mode === \"image\" && supportsImageResolutionPresets(mergedProfile.size);\r\n    const imageResolutionOptions = usesImageResolutionPicker ? buildImageResolutionOptions(mergedProfile.size.values) : [];\r\n    const activeImageResolution = usesImageResolutionPicker ? imageResolutionOption(imageResolutionOptions, props.ratio) : undefined;\r\n    const activeImageRatio = activeImageResolution?.ratio || imageRatioForSize(props.ratio) || (props.ratio.includes(\":\") ? props.ratio : \"1:1\");\r\n    const activeImageResolutionChoice: ImageResolutionChoice = activeImageResolution?.tier || \"auto\";\r\n    const imageResolutionChoiceOptions = usesImageResolutionPicker ? imageResolutionChoices(mergedProfile.size.values) : [];\r\n    const imageRatios = usesImageResolutionPicker\r\n        ? Array.from(new Set(imageResolutionOptions.filter((item) => !activeImageResolution || item.tier === activeImageResolution.tier).map((item) => item.ratio)))\r\n        : mergedProfile.size.values.length ? mergedProfile.size.values : ratioOptions.map((item) => item.value);\r\n    const ratios = props.mode === \"video\" ? props.videoProfile.ratios : imageRatios;\r\n    const referenceImageSize = props.mode === \"image\" && mergedProfile.size.allowCustom ? props.referenceImageSize : undefined;\r\n    const referenceImageSizeValue = referenceImageSize ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \"x\" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeLabel = referenceImageSize ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \" × \" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeRatio = referenceImageSize ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \":\" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeSelected = Boolean(referenceImageSizeValue && props.ratio === referenceImageSizeValue);\r\n    const resolutions = props.mode === \"video\" ? props.videoProfile.resolutions.map((value) => ({ value: value.replace(/p$/i, \"\"), label: videoResolutionLabel(value) })) : resolutionOptions;\r\n    const selectImageRatio = (nextRatio: string) => {\r\n        if (!usesImageResolutionPicker || activeImageResolutionChoice === \"auto\") {\r\n            props.setRatio(nextRatio);\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        props.setRatio(imageSizeForResolution(imageResolutionOptions, activeImageResolutionChoice, nextRatio) || nextRatio);\r\n    };\r\n    const selectImageResolution = (choice: ImageResolutionChoice) => {\r\n        if (choice === \"auto\") {\r\n            props.setRatio(mergedProfile.size.values.includes(\"auto\") ? \"auto\" : activeImageRatio);\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        const nextSize = imageSizeForResolution(imageResolutionOptions, choice, activeImageRatio) || imageResolutionOptions.find((item) => item.tier === choice)?.size;\r\n        if (nextSize) props.setRatio(nextSize);\r\n    };\r\n    const selectReferenceImageSize = () => {\r\n        if (!referenceImageSizeValue) return;\r\n        props.setRatio(referenceImageSizeValue);\r\n        setCustomRatioOpen(false);\r\n    };\r\n    const videoResolutionSupported = props.mode === \"video\" && resolutions.length > 0;\r\n    const imageSummary = [\r\n        ...(mergedProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" ? [referenceImageSizeSelected ? referenceImageSizeLabel : usesImageResolutionPicker ? formatImageResolutionSize(props.ratio, imageResolutionOptions) : props.ratio] : []),\r\n        ...(props.imageProfile.quality.supported ? [qualityLabel] : []),\r\n        ...(props.imageProfile.maxOutputs > 1 ? [props.count] : []),\r\n    ].join(\" · \");\r\n    const summary = props.mode === \"video\" ? [props.ratio, ...(videoResolutionSupported ? [videoResolutionLabel(props.videoQuality)] : [])].join(\" · \") : imageSummary;\r\n    const panel = <div className=\"creation-parameter-menu\">\r\n        {props.mode === \"video\" || mergedProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" ? <SettingSection title=\"画幅\" value={referenceImageSizeSelected ? referenceImageSizeLabel : props.mode === \"image\" && usesImageResolutionPicker ? activeImageRatio : props.ratio}><div className=\"creation-parameter-content\"><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-ratio\">{referenceImageSizeValue ? <button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={referenceImageSizeSelected} aria-label={\"使用参考图尺寸 \" + referenceImageSizeLabel} title={\"使用参考图尺寸 \" + referenceImageSizeLabel} className={\"creation-reference-size-choice\" + (referenceImageSizeSelected ? \" is-selected\" : \"\")} onClick={selectReferenceImageSize}><span className=\"creation-ratio-preview\"><span style={ratioPreviewStyle(referenceImageSizeRatio)} /></span><span>参考图</span></button> : null}{ratios.map((value) => { const selected = props.mode === \"image\" && usesImageResolutionPicker ? value === activeImageRatio : value === props.ratio; return <button key={value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={selected} className={selected ? \"is-selected\" : \"\"} onClick={() => { if (props.mode === \"image\") selectImageRatio(value); else props.setRatio(value); setCustomRatioOpen(false); }}><span className=\"creation-ratio-preview\"><span style={ratioPreviewStyle(value)} /></span><span>{value}</span></button>; })}</div>{props.mode !== \"video\" && mergedProfile.size.allowCustom && (customRatioOpen ? <label className=\"creation-custom-value\"><span>宽 x 高</span><input value={props.ratio} onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()} onChange={(event) => props.setRatio(event.target.value)} placeholder=\"1920x1080 或 2:1\" aria-label=\"自定义图片尺寸或比例\" /></label> : <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-custom-trigger\" onClick={() => setCustomRatioOpen(true)}><Plus />输入自定义尺寸</button>)}</div></SettingSection> : null}\r\n        {props.mode === \"video\" ? (videoResolutionSupported ? <SettingSection title=\"清晰度\" value={videoResolutionLabel(props.videoQuality)}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-resolution\">{resolutions.map((option) => <button key={option.value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={option.value === props.videoQuality} className={option.value === props.videoQuality ? \"自动\" : activeImageResolutionChoice.toUpperCase()}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-resolution\">{imageResolutionChoiceOptions.map((choice) => <button key={choice} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={choice === activeImageResolutionChoice} className={choice === activeImageResolutionChoice ? \"is-selected\" : \"\"} onClick={() => selectImageResolution(choice)}>{choice === \"auto\" ? \"自动\" : choice.toUpperCase()}</button>)}</div></SettingSection> : null}\r\n            {props.imageProfile.quality.supported ? <SettingSection title={activeQualityOptions.some((item) => item.value === \"1k\" || item.value === \"2k\") ? \"分辨率\" : \"图片质量\"} value={qualityLabel}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-quality\">{activeQualityOptions.map((option) => <button key={option.value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={option.value === props.quality} className={option.value === props.quality ? \"is-open\" : \"\"} /></button>\r\n    </Popover>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction SettingSection({ title, value, children }: { title: string; value?: string; children: ReactNode }) {\r\n    return <section className=\"creation-parameter-section\"><header><h3>{title}</h3>{value ? <span>{value}</span> : null}</header>{children}</section>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction DurationMenu({ profile, seconds, onChange }: { profile: VideoCapabilityConfig; seconds: string; onChange: (value: string) => void }) {\r\n    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const value = Number(normalizeVideoValue(profile, { seconds }).seconds);\r\n    const presets = profile.duration.selection === \"enum\" ? TOPAZ_VIDEO_PROTOCOLS : undefined}\r\n                        capability={mode}\r\n                        requirements={resolvedRequirements}\r\n                        onMissingConfig={() => navigateToSettings({ continueCreation: true })}\r\n                        showSelectedPrice={false}\r\n                        showOptionPrices={creditsEnabled}\r\n                        variant=\"creation\"\r\n                        showConfiguredModelName\r\n                        preserveSelection={Boolean(node.metadata?.ltvReferencePlan)}\r\n                    />\r\n                </div>\r\n                <div className=\"ml-auto flex min-w-0 shrink-0 items-center gap-1\">\r\n                    {!mediaEnhancement && <ReferenceToolsPopover\r\n                        canAutoMention={canAutoMention}\r\n                        autoLinkEnabled={autoLinkEnabled}\r\n                        onAutoMention={() => updatePrompt(autoMentionedPrompt)}\r\n                        onAutoLinkEnabledChange={setAutoLinkEnabled}\r\n                        accent={monochromeAccent}\r\n                        compact={!expanded}\r\n                    />}\r\n                    {mode === \"text\" ? (\r\n                        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-xs\" style={{ color: theme.node.text }}>\r\n                            <select aria-label=\"视频增强分辨率\" value={videoEnhancement.resolution} disabled={isRunning} className=\"h-7 rounded-md px-1\" style={{ background: theme.node.panel }}\r\n                                onChange={event => onConfigChange(node.id, { videoEnhancement: { ...videoEnhancement, resolution: event.target.value as '1080p' | '2K' | '4K' } })}>\r\n                                {['1080p','2K','4K'].map(value => <option key={value} value={value}>{value}</option>)}\r\n                            </select>\r\n                            {videoEnhancement.model === 'apo-8' && <>\r\n                                <select aria-label=\"视频增强帧率\" value={videoEnhancement.fps} disabled={isRunning} className=\"h-7 rounded-md px-1\" style={{ background: theme.node.panel }}\r\n                                    onChange={event => onConfigChange(node.id, { videoEnhancement: { ...videoEnhancement, fps: Number(event.target.value) as 24 | 30 | 60 | 90 | 120 } })}>\r\n                                    {[24,30,60,90,120].map(value => <option key={value} value={value}>{value} fps</option>)}\r\n                                </select>\r\n                                <select aria-label=\"视频增强慢放\" value={videoEnhancement.slowmo} disabled={isRunning} className=\"h-7 rounded-md px-1\" style={{ background: theme.node.panel }}\r\n                                    onChange={event => onConfigChange(node.id, { videoEnhancement: { ...videoEnhancement, slowmo: Number(event.target.value) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 5 } })}>\r\n                                    {[1,2,3,5].map(value => <option key={value} value={value}>{value === 1 ? '正常速度' : `${value} 倍慢放`}</option>)}\r\n                                </select>\r\n                            </>}\r\n                        </div>\r\n                    ) : mode === \"image\" && imageEnhancement ? (\r\n                        <label className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[var(--fs-tiny)]\" data-canvas-no-drag data-canvas-no-zoom>\r\n                            放大\r\n                            <select aria-label=\"图像增强放大倍数\" value={imageEnhancement.scale} disabled={isRunning}\r\n                                className=\"h-7 rounded-md px-1\" style={{ color: theme.node.text, background: theme.node.panel }}\r\n                                onChange={event => onConfigChange(node.id, { imageEnhancement: { ...imageEnhancement, scale: Number(event.target.value) as 2 | 4 | 6 }, count: 1 })}>\r\n                                {[2, 4, 6].map(scale => <option key={scale} value={scale}>{scale}×</option>)}\r\n                            </select>\r\n                        </label>\r\n                    ) : mode === \"image\" ?
